Rates in percent. Error sets: fixed (AC recall), wrong, kept, precision, top-1, top-3; false-positive sets: false corrections (F7 also the offer rate of the accented word and the accepted replacements by the more frequent spelling, 05c §3.7). `coll` = collision bucket (typed text valid, not counted), `flag` = flagged bucket (expected text a do-not-suggest form, not counted). E2 and E2a are judged by top-3, offer and wrong (05c §6, 0.7.1). Differences to each baseline in percentage points.
